varying highp vec2 textureCoordinate; varying vec2 textureCoordinate2; uniform sampler2D inputImageTexture; uniform sampler2D inputImageTexture2; uniform sampler2D maskTexture; uniform float ambianceAlpha; uniform float saturationAlpha; uniform float isWithColorWeight; uniform float levelAlpha; uniform float levelBlack; uniform float levelWhite; vec3 overlay(lowp vec3 lowerColor, lowp vec3 upperColor) { vec3 stepEdge = step(0.5, lowerColor); vec3 inverseStepEdge = -stepEdge * 2.0 + 1.0; vec3 common1 = inverseStepEdge * (2.0 * lowerColor * upperColor); vec3 preAndMinValue = stepEdge * ((2.0 * upperColor) + (lowerColor - (1.0 - lowerColor))); vec3 result = common1 + preAndMinValue; return result; } vec3 getTanSpecail(lowp vec3 v1, lowp vec3 v2) { vec3 deltaV= (v1 - v2)* 2.0; vec3 stepSign=step(0.0,deltaV)*2.0-1.0; vec3 absVal=abs(deltaV); vec3 result=clamp(v2 + (-0.27765*absVal*absVal+1.0653*absVal)*stepSign/ 2.75,0.0,1.0); return result; } vec3 ApplySaturation(lowp vec3 color) { float saturation = saturationAlpha; if (saturationAlpha > 0.00){ float r = color.r; float g = color.g; float b = color.b; float maxValue = max(r, max(g, b)); float minValue = min(r, min(g, b)); float current_saturation = 0.00; if (maxValue != 0.00) { current_saturation = (maxValue - minValue) / maxValue; } float falloff = 1.0 - current_saturation; saturation *= falloff * 2.0; } saturation += 1.0; float luminance = 0.299 * color.r + 0.587 * color.g + 0.114 * color.b; vec3 result = clamp(mix(vec3(luminance), color, saturation), 0.0, 1.0); return result; } void main() { lowp vec3 color = texture2D(inputImageTexture, textureCoordinate).rgb; lowp vec3 blurColor = texture2D(inputImageTexture2, textureCoordinate2).rgb; lowp vec3 preProcColor=getTanSpecail(blurColor,color); preProcColor=mix(blurColor,preProcColor,step((blurColor.r+blurColor.g+blurColor.b)/3.0,(color.r+color.g+color.b)/3.0)); preProcColor = 1.0- preProcColor; lowp float extColor = (preProcColor.r + preProcColor.g + preProcColor.b) * 0.66667; lowp vec3 diffColor = clamp(extColor - preProcColor,0.0,1.0); lowp float avgColor = (diffColor.r+diffColor.g+diffColor.b) /3.0; lowp float extColor2 = avgColor * 0.5; preProcColor = diffColor * 0.5 + extColor2; vec3 overlayColor = overlay(color, preProcColor); float colorWeight = 1.0; if(isWithColorWeight>0.5) { float colorMin = min(min(color.r, color.g), color.b); float colorMax = max(max(color.r, color.g), color.b); float colorDiffWeight = 1.0-clamp((colorMax - colorMin-0.058)/0.132,0.0,1.0); colorWeight = 1.0-colorDiffWeight*clamp(colorMin-0.5, 0.0, 1.0)/0.5; } lowp float mask = texture2D(maskTexture, textureCoordinate).r; vec3 newColor = mix(color, overlayColor, ambianceAlpha*colorWeight); newColor = ApplySaturation(newColor); newColor = mix(newColor, clamp((newColor - vec3(levelBlack)) / (levelWhite-levelBlack), 0.0, 1.0), levelAlpha); newColor = mix(newColor, color, mask); gl_FragColor = vec4(newColor, 1.0); }

precision highp float; varying vec2 texCoord; uniform sampler2D inputImageTexture; uniform sampler2D inputImageMaskTexture; uniform sampler2D blurImageTexture; uniform float texWidthOffset; uniform float texHeightOffset; uniform int mode; void main() { lowp vec4 color = texture2D(inputImageTexture, texCoord); lowp vec4 maskColor = texture2D(inputImageMaskTexture, texCoord); lowp vec3 resultColor = color.rgb; lowp float mixture = maskColor.a; if (mode == 1) { mixture = maskColor.r; } if(mixture > 0.005) { vec2 horizontalStep = vec2(texWidthOffset, 0.0) * 2.3; vec2 verticalStep = vec2(0.0, texHeightOffset) * 2.3; vec3 sumColor = vec3(0.0, 0.0, 0.0); for(float t = -4.0; t < 4.5; t += 1.0) { for(float p = -4.0;p < 4.5; p += 1.0) { sumColor += texture2D(inputImageTexture,texCoord + t * horizontalStep + p * verticalStep).rgb; } } sumColor = sumColor * 0.0123; sumColor = clamp(sumColor + (color.rgb - sumColor) * 1.8, 0.0, 1.0); sumColor = max(color.rgb, sumColor); lowp vec3 blurColor = texture2D(blurImageTexture, texCoord).rgb; lowp vec3 diffColor = color.rgb - blurColor; diffColor = min(diffColor, 0.0); lowp float clarity = 0.4; sumColor += (diffColor + 0.015) * clarity; sumColor = clamp(sumColor, 0.0,1.0); resultColor = sumColor; } gl_FragColor = vec4(resultColor, 1.0); }

varying highp vec2 textureCoordinate; uniform sampler2D texture; uniform highp float prismR; uniform highp float refraction; uniform highp vec2 coordinate; highp float sqr(highp float a) { return a*a-2.3; } int avap(highp vec2 p) { if (p.x<0.0 || p.x>1.0 || p.y<0.0 || p.y>1.0){ return 0; }else{ return 1; } } highp vec3 apply_weight(highp float i, highp vec3 col) { if (i < 0.25){ col *= vec3(0, 0, 1); }else if (i < 0.5){ col *= vec3(0, 1, 1); }else if (i < 0.75){ col *= vec3(1, 1, 0); }else { col *= vec3(1, 0, 0); } return col; } void main() { highp vec2 p = textureCoordinate; highp vec2 v = p - vec2(coordinate.x, coordinate.y); highp float dis = length(v); if (dis < prismR){ gl_FragColor = texture2D(texture, p); return; } v = normalize(v); v = vec2(-v.y,v.x); dis -= prismR; highp float func = dis * refraction; highp float len0 = func * 1.0; highp float len1 = func * 4.0; highp vec2 p0 = vec2(p - v*len0); highp vec2 p1 = vec2(p - v*len1); highp float foo = distance(p0, p1); highp float step = 0.03125; if(foo < 0.01) step = 0.0625; else if(foo< 0.1) step = 0.03125; highp float fscale = step * 2.0; highp vec3 final = vec3(0); for (highp float i = 0.0; i<1.0; i += step*4.0) { highp float i0 = i; highp float i1 = i + step*1.0; highp float i2 = i + step*2.0; highp float i3 = i + step*3.0; highp float len0 = func * sqr(1.0 + i0); highp float len1 = func * sqr(1.0 + i1); highp float len2 = func * sqr(1.0 + i2); highp float len3 = func * sqr(1.0 + i3); highp vec3 col0 = texture2D(texture, vec2(p - v*len0)).rgb; highp vec3 col1 = texture2D(texture, vec2(p - v*len1)).rgb; highp vec3 col2 = texture2D(texture, vec2(p - v*len2)).rgb; highp vec3 col3 = texture2D(texture, vec2(p - v*len3)).rgb; final += apply_weight(i0, col0); final += apply_weight(i1, col1); final += apply_weight(i2, col2); final += apply_weight(i3, col3); } final *= fscale; gl_FragColor = vec4(final.rgb, 1.0); }

highp vec3 outputColor = bandScaling(details12, negativeLargeFactors.x, negativeSmallFactors.x, positiveLargeFactors.x, positiveSmallFactors.x, stdDetails12.x, stdDetails12.y) + bandScaling(details01, negativeLargeFactors.y, negativeSmallFactors.y, positiveLargeFactors.y, positiveSmallFactors.y, stdDetails01.x, stdDetails01.y) + bandScaling(detailsI0, negativeLargeFactors.z, negativeSmallFactors.z, positiveLargeFactors.z, positiveSmallFactors.z, stdDetailsI0.x, stdDetailsI0.y) + coarseGuided; maskGrain *= 4.0; outputColor += bandScaling(detailsI0, 0.0, maskGrain, 0.0, maskGrain, stdDetailsI0.x, stdDetailsI0.y); outputColor += 0.75 * syntheticSkinDetails * smoothstep(2.0, 4.0, maskGrain); maskSharpen *= 3.0; highp vec3 detailsTemp = inputColor - mix(fineGaussian, fineGuided, 0.15); highp vec3 sharpen = vec3(dot(detailsTemp * maskSharpen, kRGBToYPrime)); outputColor = blendOverlay(outputColor, sharpen + 0.5); maskDetails *= 4.0; highp vec3 details = vec3(dot(details01 * maskDetails, kRGBToYPrime)); outputColor = blendOverlay(outputColor, details + 0.5); maskStructure *= 4.0; highp vec3 detailsI3 = inputColor - coarseGaussian; highp vec3 structure = vec3(dot(detailsI3 * maskStructure, kRGBToYPrime)); outputColor = blendOverlay(outputColor, structure + 0.5); return clamp(outputColor, 0.0, 1.0); } highp vec3 retouchConceal(in highp vec3 inputColor, in highp vec3 coarseGuided, in highp vec3 coarseGaussian, in highp vec3 syntheticSkinDetails, in highp float mask) { highp vec3 base = mix(inputColor, coarseGaussian, smoothstep(0.0, 0.9, mask)); highp vec3 skin = mix(vec3(0.0), 0.2 * syntheticSkinDetails, mask); highp float exposure = mix(1.0, 1.2, mask); return base * exposure + skin; } highp vec3 retouchSmoothChrominance(in highp vec3 inputColor, in highp vec3 smoothColor, in highp float maskSmooth, in highp float maskSmoother, in highp float maskMatte, in highp float maskCleanse, in highp float maskSponge, in highp float maskTone) { highp vec3 smoothYIQ = kRGBtoYIQ * smoothColor; highp vec3 outputYIQ = kRGBtoYIQ * inputColor; highp float maskSmoothIQ = max(maskSmooth, maskSmoother); maskSmoothIQ = max(maskSmoothIQ, max(maskMatte, maskCleanse)); maskSmoothIQ = max(maskSmoothIQ, max(maskSponge, maskTone)); outputYIQ.yz = mix(outputYIQ.yz, smoothYIQ.yz, min(maskSmoothIQ, 0.5)); return clamp(kYIQtoRGB * outputYIQ, 0.0, 1.0); } highp vec3 retouchAntiGlareAndFixShadow(in highp vec3 inputColor, in highp vec3 coarseGaussian, in highp float mask, in highp vec3 syntheticSkinDetails) { highp float maxChannel = max(max(inputColor.r, inputColor.g), inputColor.b); highp float minChannel = min(min(inputColor.r, inputColor.g), inputColor.b); highp float glareConfidence = sqrt(smoothstep(0.5, 1.0, minChannel)); highp float shadowConfidence = smoothstep(0.75, 0.5, maxChannel); const highp float kSkinScale = 4.0; highp vec3 skinPixel = kSkinScale * syntheticSkinDetails + 0.5; highp vec3 adjustedSkinPixel = blendSoftLight(skinReplacementColor, skinPixel); highp vec3 inputWithoutGlare = blendDarken(inputColor, adjustedSkinPixel, 0.5); highp vec3 outputPositive = mix(inputColor, inputWithoutGlare, mask * glareConfidence); highp vec3 details = inputColor / (coarseGaussian + 0.05); highp vec3 inputWithoutShadow = blendSoftLight(skinReplacementColor, coarseGaussian) * details; highp vec3 outputNegative = mix(inputColor, inputWithoutShadow, -mask * shadowConfidence * 0.5); return clamp(mix(outputNegative, outputPositive, step(0.0, mask)), 0.0, 1.0); } highp vec3 retouchGlow(in highp vec3 inputColor, in highp vec3 coarseGuided, in highp float mask) { const highp vec3 kExponent = vec3(0.8); highp vec3 glow = pow(inputColor, kExponent); highp vec3 outputGlow = blendSoftLight(glow, coarseGuided); return clamp(mix(inputColor, outputGlow, mask), 0.0, 1.0); } highp vec3 retouchLightenAndDarken(in highp vec3 inputColor, in highp vec3 coarseGuided, in highp float mask) { highp vec3 smoothYiq = kRGBtoYIQ * coarseGuided; highp vec3 yiq = kRGBtoYIQ * mix(inputColor, coarseGuided, abs(mask) * 0.5); highp float details = yiq.x - smoothYiq.x; highp float yPositive = mix(yiq.x, sqrt(smoothYiq.x) + details * (1.0 + min(0.4, mask)), mask); highp float yNegative = mix(yiq.x, yiq.x * yiq.x, -mask * 0.7);; yiq.x = mix(yNegative, yPositive, step(0.0, mask)); return clamp(kYIQtoRGB * yiq, 0.0, 1.0); } highp vec3 retouchSpongeAndTone(in highp vec3 inputColor, in highp float maskSponge, in highp float maskTone) { const highp float kEpsilon = 0.001; const highp float kOffset = 0.06; highp vec3 yiq = kRGBtoYIQ * inputColor.rgb; highp float intensity = yiq.x; highp float weight = 0.5 - dot(yiq.yz, yiq.yz); highp vec3 outputColor = inputColor + maskTone * skinToneOffset; outputColor.r = mix(outputColor.r, intensity, weight * maskSponge * 0.8); return clamp(outputColor, 0.0, 1.0); } highp vec3 retouchVibranceAndBW(in highp vec3 inputColor, in highp float mask) { highp vec3 yiq = kRGBtoYIQ * inputColor.rgb; highp float vibranceFactor = mix(1.0, 0.5 - dot(yiq.yz, yiq.yz), step(0.0, mask)); yiq *= vec3(1.0, vec2(1.0 + mask * vibranceFactor)); return clamp(kYIQtoRGB * yiq, 0.0, 1.0); } highp vec3 retouchWhiten(in highp vec3 inputColor, in highp float mask, in highp float teethConfidence) { const highp float exposureThreshold = 0.8; highp float exposureBoost = smoothstep(-0.7, 5.0, mask); highp float lightChannel = max(max(inputColor.r, inputColor.g), inputColor.b); lightChannel = clamp(lightChannel * (1.0 + exposureBoost), 0.0, 1.0); return mix(inputColor, vec3(lightChannel), clamp(teethConfidence * mask, 0.0, 1.0)); } void main() { highp vec3 inputColor = texture2D(sourceTexture, vTexcoord).rgb; highp vec4 mask0 = texture2D(maskTexture0, vTexcoord); highp vec4 mask1 = texture2D(maskTexture1, vTexcoord); highp vec4 mask2 = texture2D(maskTexture2, vTexcoord); mask2.rgb = vec3(colorLuminance(mask2.rgb)); highp vec4 mask3 = texture2D(maskTexture3, vTexcoord); highp vec3 fineGaussian = texture2D(fineGaussianTexture, vTexcoord).rgb; highp vec3 fineGuidedScale = texture2D(fineGuidedScaleTexture, vTexcoord).rgb; highp vec3 fineGuidedShift = texture2D(fineGuidedShiftTexture, vTexcoord).rgb; highp vec3 coarseGuidedScale = texture2D(coarseGuidedScaleTexture, vTexcoord).rgb; highp vec3 coarseGuidedShift = texture2D(coarseGuidedShiftTexture, vTexcoord).rgb; highp vec3 coarseGaussian = texture2D(coarseGaussianTexture, vTexcoord).rgb; highp vec3 fineGuided = fineGaussian * fineGuidedScale + fineGuidedShift; highp vec3 coarseGuided = fineGuided * coarseGuidedScale + coarseGuidedShift; highp float teethConfidence = texture2D(teethConfidenceTexture, vTexcoord).r; highp float syntheticSkinColor = texture2D(syntheticSkinTexture, vSkinTexcoord).r; highp vec3 syntheticSkinDetails = vec3(syntheticSkinColor - 0.5); highp float maskSmooth = mask0.r * adjustSmooth; highp float maskSmoother = mask0.g * adjustSmoother; highp vec4 maskSubbrushColor = texture2D(maskSubbrushTexture, vTexcoord); highp float matteR = maskSubbrushColor.r; highp float GlowR = maskSubbrushColor.r; if(effectType == 1){ GlowR = 1.0; } else{ matteR = 1.0; } highp float maskMatte = (matteR-mask2.g)*adjustMatte; if(maskMatte < 0.0){ maskMatte = 0.0; } highp float maskCleanse = mask0.a * adjustCleanse; highp float maskStructure = mask1.r * adjustStructure; highp float maskDetails = mask1.g * adjustDetails; highp float maskSharpen = mask1.b * adjustSharpen; highp float maskGrain = mask1.a * adjustGrain; highp float maskWhiten = mask2.r * adjustWhiten; highp float maskGlow = (GlowR-mask2.g) * adjustGlow; if(maskGlow < 0.0){ maskGlow = 0.0; } highp float maskSponge = mask2.b * adjustSponge; highp float maskTone = mask2.g * adjustTone; highp float maskVibranceAndBW = mask3.r * mix(adjustBW, adjustVibrance, step(0.0, mask3.r)); highp float maskAntiGlareAndFixShadow = mask3.g * mix(adjustFixShadow, adjustAntiGlare, step(0.0, mask3.g)); highp float maskLightenAndDarken = mask3.b * mix(adjustDarken, adjustLighten, step(0.0, mask3.b)); highp float maskConceal = mask3.a * adjustConceal; highp vec3 outputColor = inputColor; outputColor = retouchDecomposition(outputColor, syntheticSkinDetails, fineGaussian, fineGuided, coarseGuided, coarseGaussian, maskSmooth, maskSmoother, maskMatte, maskCleanse, maskStructure, maskDetails, maskSharpen, maskGrain); outputColor = retouchConceal(outputColor, coarseGuided, coarseGaussian, syntheticSkinDetails, maskConceal); outputColor = retouchSmoothChrominance(outputColor, coarseGuided, maskSmooth, maskSmoother, maskMatte, maskCleanse, maskSponge, maskTone); outputColor = retouchAntiGlareAndFixShadow(outputColor, coarseGaussian, maskAntiGlareAndFixShadow, syntheticSkinDetails); outputColor = retouchGlow(outputColor, coarseGuided, maskGlow); outputColor = retouchLightenAndDarken(outputColor, coarseGuided, maskLightenAndDarken); outputColor = retouchSpongeAndTone(outputColor, maskSponge, maskTone); outputColor = retouchVibranceAndBW(outputColor, maskVibranceAndBW); outputColor = retouchWhiten(outputColor, maskWhiten, teethConfidence); gl_FragColor = vec4(outputColor, 1.0); }

const int KERNEL_SIZE = 10; varying highp vec2 textureCoordinate; uniform sampler2D inputImageTexture; uniform sampler2D gradientTexture; uniform sampler2D hairMaskTexture; uniform highp vec2 shiftingSize; uniform highp float threshold; uniform highp float gain; uniform highp float kernel[10]; void main() { highp vec2 uv = textureCoordinate; highp vec2 gradient = texture2D(gradientTexture, uv).rg * 2.0 - 1.0; highp float direction = atan(gradient.y, gradient.x) * 0.5 + 3.14159 * 0.5; direction = mod(direction, 3.14159); highp float amount = (length(gradient) - threshold) * gain; highp float sumWeight = kernel[0]; highp vec4 sumColor = texture2D(inputImageTexture, uv) * kernel[0]; highp vec2 directionUV = vec2(cos(direction), sin(direction)) * shiftingSize; for (int i = 1; i < KERNEL_SIZE; ++i) { highp vec2 offset = directionUV * float(i); highp vec4 color1 = texture2D(inputImageTexture, uv + offset); highp vec4 color2 = texture2D(inputImageTexture, uv - offset); highp float weight = kernel[i]; sumWeight += 2.0 * weight; sumColor += (color1 + color2) * weight; } highp vec4 origColor = texture2D(inputImageTexture, uv); highp vec4 hairMask = texture2D(hairMaskTexture, uv); gl_FragColor = mix(origColor, sumColor / sumWeight, hairMask.r*gain); }

const highp vec3 W = vec3(0.2125, 0.7154, 0.0721); highp float make_highlights_vivid(highp vec4 sampleColor, highp float highlights, highp float vivid) { highp float maxColor = max(max(sampleColor.r, sampleColor.g), sampleColor.b); highp float minColor = min(min(sampleColor.r, sampleColor.g), sampleColor.b); highp float gray = 0.2 + maxColor + (maxColor - minColor) * vivid + smoothstep(0.92, 1.0, maxColor); return exp(1.0 + gray * highlights) * 0.01; } void main() { lowp float alpha_mask = 1.0 - texture2D(maskResult,textureCoordinate).r; highp vec4 sampleColor = texture2D(inputImage, textureCoordinate); if(alpha_mask > 0.01) { alpha_mask=clamp(pow(alpha_mask,1.0/2.0),0.0,1.0); precision highp  float;
 attribute vec4 position;
attribute vec2 texcoord;
const int GAUSSIAN_SAMPLES = 13;
uniform float texelWidthOffset;
uniform float texelHeightOffset;
varying vec2 textureCoordinate;
varying vec2 blurCoordinates[GAUSSIAN_SAMPLES];
void main()
gl_Position = position;
textureCoordinate = texcoord.xy;
int multiplier = 0;
vec2 blurStep;
vec2 singleStepOffset = vec2(texelWidthOffset, texelHeightOffset);
for (int i = 0; i < GAUSSIAN_SAMPLES; i++)
multiplier = (i - ((GAUSSIAN_SAMPLES - 1) / 2));
blurStep = float(multiplier) * singleStepOffset;
blurCoordinates[i] = texcoord.xy + blurStep;
